> AGENT

Yes. Current state:

- ArtAgents already has the “custom/agent node” concept in [artagents/nodes](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/nodes/registry.py:1). It loads built-ins plus local curated JSON manifests like [moirae.json](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/nodes/curated/moirae.json:1).
- It does not currently fetch nodes from Banodoco/Reigh DB, install repos, or know which external nodes are mandatory/default.
- The Reigh app’s reusable pattern is `resources`: rows have `type`, `metadata`, `is_public`, owner, and embedded sample media for presets. See [useResources.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/features/resources/hooks/useResources.ts:1) and preset media handling in [submitPreset.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/PhaseConfigSelectorModal/hooks/submitPreset.ts:1).
- The worker already has a plugin installer path in [plugins.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker/Wan2GP/shared/utils/plugins.py:156), but its `default_plugins = {}` is empty. That is the likely hook for “install required/default nodes” on startup.

Recommended shape:

1. Add a dedicated `agent_nodes` table, not another `resources` type.
   It should have fields like `slug`, `node_id`, `name`, `description`, `repo_url`, `manifest_path` or `manifest_json`, `author_user_id`, `discord_user_id`, `discord_username`, `is_public`, `is_featured`, `install_by_default`, `default_install_order`, `install_enabled`, `version`, `metadata`.

2. Add `agent_node_media` as a separate media table.
   This is cleaner than embedding sample media JSON because nodes may have multiple creator/demo videos and likely need browsing/filtering later:
   `agent_node_id`, `user_id`, `media_type`, `url`, `thumbnail_url`, `alt_text`, `position`, `is_primary`.

3. Reuse the existing resource UI ideas, but make agent-node-specific hooks/components.
   The form should feel like the phase-config/Lora form: creator, description, public toggle, repo URL, sample images/videos, primary media. The upload path can reuse `uploadImageToStorage` initially.

4. Add a website API/RPC for default installables:
   Query public, enabled rows where `install_by_default = true`, ordered by `default_install_order`. That is the “mandatory nodes” source of truth.

5. Add installer support:
   - In ArtAgents: add `pipeline.py nodes install-defaults` or startup sync that fetches `agent_nodes`, clones/pulls pinned repos, validates manifests with the existing node schema, and loads installed manifests after built-ins/curated nodes.
   - In reigh-worker/Wan2GP: replace the empty `default_plugins = {}` path with DB-backed default plugin/node rows, then call the existing `install_plugin_from_url`.

Key naming: I’d call the default flag `install_by_default` or `required_by_default`, not just `default`, because it distinguishes “featured in UI” from “automatically installed.” `is_featured` should only control promotion/display.

Does that plan look good to you?

> AGENT

Mostly yes. The corrected plan is much better: it treats the Banodoco website database as the source of truth, keeps agent nodes out of `resources`, makes ArtAgents consume the website API, and scopes install behavior to ArtAgents default/mandatory targets instead of dragging in unrelated Reigh worker plugin assumptions.

The parts I’d still watch before implementation:

- Service/admin control of `featured`, `default`, and `mandatory` needs real DB enforcement, not just hidden UI.
- The install target format needs to be nailed down: repo URL plus ref/path/manifest entry is probably enough for v1.
- Media storage should reuse existing website patterns only if public browsing works cleanly; otherwise signed URLs need to be explicit.
- Mandatory install failure behavior should be conservative: don’t break built-in nodes unless the missing mandatory node is actually required for a workflow.

The critique pass is still running, so I’ll use that to catch anything concrete before execution.

> DEVELOPER

Does the plan look good?

> AGENT

Yes, v2 looks good directionally.

The important fixes are in place now:

- Banodoco website DB is the source of truth.
- Agent nodes stay separate from `resources`.
- Website exposes the catalog API for ArtAgents.
- API method/auth is explicit instead of accidentally depending on existing POST/auth defaults.
- RLS/read policies include safe public catalog metadata for badges/defaults.
- ArtAgents DB-backed loading is controlled centrally so CLI and runner behavior stay consistent.
- V1 installer scope is concrete: `manifest_url` and `archive_url`, no git clone/ref support yet.

One tradeoff: avoiding git install support in v1 is conservative. It reduces implementation risk, but if most “agent nodes” are actually repos rather than downloadable manifests/archives, we may need git support next. I’d still keep v1 narrow unless you know repo cloning is mandatory immediately.

Critique is still running; I’ll keep going and let the gate decide if anything remains blocking.

> AGENT

The website side now has a public `agent-node-catalog` Edge Function and the Supabase gateway entry that keeps it unauthenticated. I’m moving to ArtAgents next: no network by default, explicit Banodoco opt-in, git clone/fetch support, and manifest ID verification before registration.

storage_path: string;\n+  mime_type: string;\n+  file_size_bytes: number;\n+  width: number | null;\n+  height: number | null;\n+  duration_seconds: number | null;\n+  alt_text: string | null;\n+  caption: string | null;\n+  display_order: number;\n+  created_at: string;\n+};\n+\n+function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {\n+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\n+    status,\n+    headers: JSON_HEADERS,\n+  });\n+}\n+\n+function chosenRef(target: InstallTargetRow): Record<string, string> {\n+  if (target.commit_sha) return { commit_sha: target.commit_sha };\n+  if (target.tag) return { tag: target.tag };\n+  if (target.branch) return { branch: target.branch };\n+  if (target.source_ref) return { source_ref: target.source_ref };\n+  return {};\n+}\n+\n+function byNodeId<T extends { agent_node_id: string }>(rows: T[]): Map<string, T[]> {\n+  const grouped = new Map<string, T[]>();\n+  for (const row of rows) {\n+    const existing = grouped.get(row.agent_node_id);\n+    if (existing) {\n+      existing.push(row);\n+    } else {\n+      grouped.set(row.agent_node_id, [row]);\n+    }\n+  }\n+  return grouped;\n+}\n+\n+serve((req) => {\n+  return withEdgeRequest(req, {\n+    functionName: \"agent-node-catalog\",\n+    logPrefix: \"[AGENT-NODE-CATALOG]\",\n+    method: \"GET\",\n+    parseBody: \"none\",\n+    auth: { required: false },\n+  }, async ({ supabaseAdmin, logger }) => {\n+    const { data: nodes, error: nodeError } = await supabaseAdmin\n+      .from(\"public_agent_node_catalog\")\n+      .select(\"*\")\n+      .order(\"catalog_rank\", { ascending: true })\n+      .order(\"name\", { ascending: true });\n+\n+    if (nodeError) {\n+      logger.error(\"Failed to fetch public agent node catalog\", { error: nodeError.message });\n+      return jsonResponse({ error: \"Failed to fetch agent node catalog\" }, 500);\n+    }\n+\n+    const { data: targets, error: targetError } = await supabaseAdmin\n+      .from(\"public_agent_node_install_targets\")\n+      .select(\"*\")\n+      .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true });\n+\n+    if (targetError) {\n+      logger.error(\"Failed to fetch public agent node install targets\", { error: targetError.message });\n+      return jsonResponse({ error: \"Failed to fetch agent node install targets\" }, 500);\n+    }\n+\n+    const { data: media, error: mediaError } = await supabaseAdmin\n+      .from(\"public_agent_node_media\")\n+      .select(\"*\")\n+      .order(\"display_order\", { ascending: true })\n+      .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true });\n+\n+    if (mediaError) {\n+      logger.error(\"Failed to fetch public agent node media\", { error: mediaError.message });\n+      return jsonResponse({ error: \"Failed to fetch agent node media\" }, 500);\n+    }\n+\n+    const targetsByNode = byNodeId((targets ?? []) as InstallTargetRow[]);\n+    const mediaByNode = byNodeId((media ?? []) as MediaRow[]);\n+    const payloadNodes = ((nodes ?? []) as CatalogNodeRow[]).map((node) => ({\n+      id: node.id,\n+      slug: node.slug,\n+      name: node.name,\n+      short_description: node.short_description,\n+      description: node.description,\n+      repo_url: node.repo_url,\n+      expected_manifest_id: node.expected_manifest_id,\n+      creator: {\n+        discord_id: node.creator_discord_id,\n+        display_name: node.creator_display_name,\n+      },\n+      catalog: {\n+        featured: node.is_featured,\n+        default: node.is_default,\n+        mandatory: node.is_mandatory,\n+        rank: node.catalog_rank,\n+        label: node.catalog_label,\n+        summary: node.catalog_summary,\n+      },\n+      install_targets: (targetsByNode.get(node.id) ?? []).map((target) => ({\n+        id: target.id,\n+        label: target.label,\n+        source_type: target.source_type,\n+        repo_url: target.repo_url,\n+        manifest_url: target.manifest_url,\n+        archive_url: target.archive_url,\n+        manifest_path: target.manifest_path,\n+        expected_node_id: target.expected_node_id,\n+        install_subdir: target.install_subdir,\n+        ref: chosenRef(target),\n+      })),\n+      media: (mediaByNode.get(node.id) ?? []).map((item) => ({\n+        id: item.id,\n+        type: item.media_type,\n+        bucket: item.storage_bucket,\n+        path: item.storage_path,\n+        mime_type: item.mime_type,\n+        file_size_bytes: item.file_size_bytes,\n+        width: item.width,\n+        height: item.height,\n+        duration_seconds: item.duration_seconds,\n+        alt_text: item.alt_text,\n+        caption: item.caption,\n+      })),\n+      created_at: node.created_at,\n+      updated_at: node.updated_at,\n+    }));\n+\n+    return jsonResponse({\n+      nodes: payloadNodes,\n+      default_node_ids: payloadNodes.filter((node) => node.catalog.default).map((node) => node.expected_manifest_id),\n+      mandatory_node_ids: payloadNodes.filter((node) => node.catalog.mandatory).map((node) => node.expected_manifest_id),\n+    });\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> AGENT

Implemented the core slice of the Banodoco database-backed agent node plan, including git support.

What changed:
- Added dedicated Banodoco website DB schema for `agent_nodes`, catalog metadata, git/install targets, and media, with RLS, public-safe views, owner/service-role split, media storage constraints, and exact-one git ref validation: [migration](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260430192700_create_agent_node_tables.sql:26)
- Added unauthenticated public `GET` Edge Function `agent-node-catalog` and Supabase config entry: [function](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/functions/agent-node-catalog/index.ts:95), [config](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/supabase/config.toml:56)
- Added frontend catalog client/types/hooks for the website: [api.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/features/agent-nodes/api.ts:4), [useAgentNodeCatalog.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/features/agent-nodes/hooks/useAgentNodeCatalog.ts:9)
- Added ArtAgents opt-in Banodoco catalog loading with no network by default: [banodoco_catalog.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/nodes/banodoco_catalog.py:21)
- Added git-backed manifest fetch/install target support with exact ref validation and manifest ID mismatch rejection: [install.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/nodes/install.py:24)
- Wired CLI/env opt-in flags like `--banodoco-agent-nodes` and `ARTAGENTS_BANODOCO_CATALOG_URL`: [cli.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents/artagents/nodes/cli.py:30)

Verification:
- `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_nodes*.py'` passed: 38 tests.
- Targeted website lint passed: `npx eslint src/features/agent-nodes supabase/functions/agent-node-catalog/index.ts --ext ts --max-warnings 0`.
- `supabase db lint --local` could not run because local Postgres on `127.0.0.1:54322` is not running.
- Repo-wide `npm run lint:edge` still fails on existing unrelated lint debt outside this change.

I deployed the subagent, but it didn’t finish in time, so I shut it down and implemented the website slice locally.

Done now:
- Added `node_type` to the DB/API contract: `agent` vs `orchestrator`.
- Added `/agents` route inside the authenticated app.
- Added an Agents item to the tools pane.
- Built the Agents catalog page with:
  - `Agent` / `Orchestrator` badges
  - `Default`, `Mandatory`, `Featured` badges
  - media preview support
  - creator info
  - repo link
  - git install target/ref details

'border-cyan-300/50 text-cyan-100' : 'border-emerald-300/50 text-emerald-100',\n+      )}\n+    >\n+      {isOrchestrator ? <Workflow className=\"h-3 w-3\" /> : <Bot className=\"h-3 w-3\" />}\n+      {isOrchestrator ? 'Orchestrator' : 'Agent'}\n+    </Badge>\n+  );\n+}\n+\n+function AgentNodeMiniCard({ node }: { node: AgentNodeCatalogItem }) {\n+  const firstTarget = node.install_targets[0];\n+  const creator = node.creator.display_name || node.creator.discord_id;\n+\n+  return (\n+    <article className=\"flex h-full flex-col rounded-lg border border-[#ecede3]/15 bg-[#031407]/85 p-4 shadow-[0_18px_60px_rgba(0,0,0,0.28)] backdrop-blur\">\n+      <div className=\"mb-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2\">\n+        <AgentNodeBadge node={node} />\n+        {node.catalog.mandatory && (\n+          <Badge className=\"gap-1 bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-600\">\n+            <ShieldCheck className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n+            Mandatory\n+          </Badge>\n+        )}\n+        {node.catalog.default && (\n+          <Badge className=\"gap-1 bg-blue-600 text-white hover:bg-blue-600\">\n+            <PackageCheck className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n+            Default\n+          </Badge>\n+        )}\n+        {node.catalog.featured && (\n+          <Badge className=\"gap-1 bg-amber-400 text-black hover:bg-amber-400\">\n+            <Star className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n+            Featured\n+          </Badge>\n+        )}\n+      </div>\n+\n+      <div className=\"min-w-0\">\n+        <h3 className=\"text-lg font-theme-heading text-[#ecede3]\">{node.name}</h3>\n+        {creator && <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-[#ecede3]/55\">by {creator}</p>}\n+      </div>\n+\n+      <p className=\"mt-3 flex-1 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#ecede3]/72 line-clamp-3\">\n+        {nodeSummary(node)}\n+      </p>\n+\n+      <div className=\"mt-4 rounded-md border border-[#ecede3]/10 bg-black/20 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-[#ecede3]/60\">\n+        {firstTarget ? (\n+          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3\">\n+            <span className=\"flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1\">\n+              <GitBranch className=\"h-3 w-3 shrink-0\" />\n+              <span className=\"truncate\">{firstTarget.manifest_path || firstTarget.expected_node_id}</span>\n+            </span>\n+            <code className=\"shrink-0 rounded bg-[#ecede3]/10 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] text-[#ecede3]/70\">\n+              {refLabel(firstTarget)}\n+            </code>\n+          </div>\n+        ) : (\n+          'Install target pending review'\n+        )}\n+      </div>\n+    </article>\n+  );\n+}\n+\n+export function HomeAgentNodesSection() {\n+  const { data, isLoading, isFetching, error, refetch } = useAgentNodeCatalog();\n+  const nodes = data?.nodes ?? [];\n+  const previewNodes = nodes.slice(0, 6);\n+  const orchestrators = nodes.filter((node) => node.node_type === 'orchestrator').length;\n+  const agents = nodes.length - orchestrators;\n+\n+  return (\n+    <section id=\"agents\" className=\"relative z-10 border-t border-[#ecede3]/10 bg-[#010E00]/90 px-4 py-14 md:py-18\">\n+      <div className=\"mx-auto max-w-6xl\">\n+        <div className=\"mb-8 flex flex-col gap-5 md:flex-row md:items-end md:justify-between\">\n+          <div>\n+            <div className=\"mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[#ecede3]/50\">\n+              <Bot className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n+              Agent nodes\n+            </div>\n+            <h2 className=\"text-3xl font-theme-heading text-[#ecede3] md:text-4xl\">Community agents for Reigh</h2>\n+            <p className=\"mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#ecede3]/70 md:text-base\">\n+              Banodoco catalog entries for installable git-backed nodes, including the default and mandatory nodes ArtAgents can pull in automatically.\n+            </p>\n+          </div>\n+\n+          <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n+            <Badge variant=\"outline\" className=\"border-cyan-300/40 bg-white/10 text-cyan-100\">{orchestrators} Orchestrators</Badge>\n+            <Badge variant=\"outline\" className=\"border-emerald-300/40 bg-white/10 text-emerald-100\">{agents} Agents</Badge>\n+            <Button\n+              variant=\"outline\"\n+              size=\"sm\"\n+              onClick={() => void refetch()}\n+              disabled={isFetching}\n+              className=\"gap-2 border-[#ecede3]/25 bg-transparent text-[#ecede3] hover:bg-[#ecede3]/10 hover:text-[#ecede3]\"\n+            >\n+              <RefreshCw className={cn('h-4 w-4', isFetching && 'animate-spin')} />\n+              Refresh\n+            </Button>\n+          </div>\n+        </div>\n+\n+        {isLoading ? (\n+          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3\">\n+            {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, index) => (\n+              <div key={index} className=\"h-56 animate-pulse rounded-lg border border-[#ecede3]/10 bg-white/10\" />\n+            ))}\n+          </div>\n+        ) : error ? (\n+          <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-red-400/30 bg-red-950/40 p-5 text-sm text-red-100\">\n+            {error.message}\n+          </div>\n+        ) : previewNodes.length === 0 ? (\n+          <div className=\"rounded-lg border border-[#ecede3]/15 bg-[#031407]/85 p-8 text-center text-[#ecede3]\">\n+            <Bot className=\"mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-[#ecede3]/45\" />\n+            <h3 className=\"mt-4 text-xl font-theme-heading\">No approved agent nodes yet</h3>\n+            <p className=\"mx-auto mt-2 max-w-xl text-sm text-[#ecede3]/65\">\n+              Once submissions are approved in the Banodoco database, they will appear here with Agent or Orchestrator badges.\n+            </p>\n+          </div>\n+        ) : (\n+          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3\">\n+            {previewNodes.map((node) => (\n+              <AgentNodeMiniCard key={node.id} node={node} />\n+            ))}\n+          </div>\n+        )}\n+\n+        <div className=\"mt-7 flex justify-center\">\n+          <Button asChild className=\"gap-2 bg-[#ecede3] text-[#010E00] hover:bg-[#ecede3]/90\">\n+            <Link to=\"/2rp/agents\">\n+              Open full catalog\n+              <ExternalLink className=\"h-4 w-4\" />\n+            </Link>\n+          </Button>\n+        </div>\n+      </div>\n+    </section>\n+  );\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

I fixed it now:

- `/2rp` renders the website Home page with a new Agents section added below the hero.
- `/2rp/agents` renders the full catalog page.
- `/agents` still works inside the app layout.
- The Home section shows `Orchestrator` vs `Agent` badges on each item, plus `Default`, `Mandatory`, and `Featured` badges.

- [HomePage.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/pages/Home/HomePage.tsx)

- Route tests pass
- New Agents section component test passes

  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-website/src/features/agentNodes/useAgentNodeCatalog.ts\n+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n+import { fetchAgentNodeCatalog, logAgentNodesDebug } from './api';\n+import type { AgentNodeCatalogResponse } from './types';\n+\n+interface UseAgentNodeCatalogResult {\n+  data: AgentNodeCatalogResponse | null;\n+  loading: boolean;\n+  error: string | null;\n+  refresh: () => void;\n+}\n+\n+export function useAgentNodeCatalog(): UseAgentNodeCatalogResult {\n+  const [data, setData] = useState<AgentNodeCatalogResponse | null>(null);\n+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n+  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n+  const [refreshNonce, setRefreshNonce] = useState(0);\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    const controller = new AbortController();\n+\n+    setLoading(true);\n+    setError(null);\n+\n+    fetchAgentNodeCatalog(controller.signal)\n+      .then((catalog) => {\n+        setData(catalog);\n+        logAgentNodesDebug('catalog hook loaded', { nodeCount: catalog.nodes.length });\n+      })\n+      .catch((caught: unknown) => {\n+        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;\n+        const message = caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'Failed to load agent nodes.';\n+        setError(message);\n+        logAgentNodesDebug('catalog hook error', { message });\n+      })\n+      .finally(() => {\n+        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);\n+      });\n+\n+    return () => controller.abort();\n+  }, [refreshNonce]);\n+\n+  return {\n+    data,\n+    loading,\n+    error,\n+    refresh: () => setRefreshNonce((value) => value + 1),\n+  };\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

created_at: string;\n+};\n+\n+type MediaRow = {\n+  id: string;\n+  agent_node_id: string;\n+  media_type: \"image\" | \"video\";\n+  storage_bucket: string;\n+  storage_path: string;\n+  mime_type: string;\n+  file_size_bytes: number;\n+  width: number | null;\n+  height: number | null;\n+  duration_seconds: number | null;\n+  alt_text: string | null;\n+  caption: string | null;\n+  display_order: number;\n+  created_at: string;\n+};\n+\n+function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {\n+  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: JSON_HEADERS });\n+}\n+\n+function chosenRef(target: InstallTargetRow): Record<string, string> {\n+  if (target.commit_sha) return { commit_sha: target.commit_sha };\n+  if (target.tag) return { tag: target.tag };\n+  if (target.branch) return { branch: target.branch };\n+  if (target.source_ref) return { source_ref: target.source_ref };\n+  return {};\n+}\n+\n+function byNodeId<T extends { agent_node_id: string }>(rows: T[]): Map<string, T[]> {\n+  const grouped = new Map<string, T[]>();\n+  for (const row of rows) {\n+    const existing = grouped.get(row.agent_node_id);\n+    if (existing) existing.push(row);\n+    else grouped.set(row.agent_node_id, [row]);\n+  }\n+  return grouped;\n+}\n+\n+Deno.serve(async (req) => {\n+  if (req.method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n+    return new Response(\"ok\", { headers: JSON_HEADERS });\n+  }\n+  if (req.method !== \"GET\") {\n+    return jsonResponse({ error: \"Method not allowed\" }, 405);\n+  }\n+\n+  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_URL\");\n+  const serviceKey = Deno.env.get(\"SB_SECRET_KEY\") ?? Deno.env.get(\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\");\n+  if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceKey) {\n+    return jsonResponse({ error: \"Supabase service credentials are not configured\" }, 500);\n+  }\n+\n+  const supabase = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceKey);\n+\n+  const { data: nodes, error: nodeError } = await supabase\n+    .from(\"public_agent_node_catalog\")\n+    .select(\"*\")\n+    .order(\"catalog_rank\", { ascending: true })\n+    .order(\"name\", { ascending: true });\n+\n+  if (nodeError) {\n+    console.error(\"[agent-node-catalog] nodes failed\", nodeError.message);\n+    return jsonResponse({ error: \"Failed to fetch agent node catalog\" }, 500);\n+  }\n+\n+  const { data: targets, error: targetError } = await supabase\n+    .from(\"public_agent_node_install_targets\")\n+    .select(\"*\")\n+    .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true });\n+\n+  if (targetError) {\n+    console.error(\"[agent-node-catalog] targets failed\", targetError.message);\n+    return jsonResponse({ error: \"Failed to fetch agent node install targets\" }, 500);\n+  }\n+\n+  const { data: media, error: mediaError } = await supabase\n+    .from(\"public_agent_node_media\")\n+    .select(\"*\")\n+    .order(\"display_order\", { ascending: true })\n+    .order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true });\n+\n+  if (mediaError) {\n+    console.error(\"[agent-node-catalog] media failed\", mediaError.message);\n+    return jsonResponse({ error: \"Failed to fetch agent node media\" }, 500);\n+  }\n+\n+  const targetsByNode = byNodeId((targets ?? []) as InstallTargetRow[]);\n+  const mediaByNode = byNodeId((media ?? []) as MediaRow[]);\n+  const payloadNodes = ((nodes ?? []) as CatalogNodeRow[]).map((node) => ({\n+    id: node.id,\n+    slug: node.slug,\n+    name: node.name,\n+    node_type: node.node_type ?? \"agent\",\n+    short_description: node.short_description,\n+    description: node.description,\n+    repo_url: node.repo_url,\n+    expected_manifest_id: node.expected_manifest_id,\n+    creator: {\n+      discord_id: node.creator_discord_id,\n+      display_name: node.creator_display_name,\n+    },\n+    catalog: {\n+      featured: node.is_featured,\n+      default: node.is_default,\n+      mandatory: node.is_mandatory,\n+      rank: node.catalog_rank,\n+      label: node.catalog_label,\n+      summary: node.catalog_summary,\n+    },\n+    install_targets: (targetsByNode.get(node.id) ?? []).map((target) => ({\n+      id: target.id,\n+      label: target.label,\n+      source_type: target.source_type,\n+      repo_url: target.repo_url,\n+      manifest_url: target.manifest_url,\n+      archive_url: target.archive_url,\n+      manifest_path: target.manifest_path,\n+      expected_node_id: target.expected_node_id,\n+      install_subdir: target.install_subdir,\n+      ref: chosenRef(target),\n+    })),\n+    media: (mediaByNode.get(node.id) ?? []).map((item) => ({\n+      id: item.id,\n+      type: item.media_type,\n+      bucket: item.storage_bucket,\n+      path: item.storage_path,\n+      mime_type: item.mime_type,\n+      file_size_bytes: item.file_size_bytes,\n+      width: item.width,\n+      height: item.height,\n+      duration_seconds: item.duration_seconds,\n+      alt_text: item.alt_text,\n+      caption: item.caption,\n+    })),\n+    created_at: node.created_at,\n+    updated_at: node.updated_at,\n+  }));\n+\n+  return jsonResponse({\n+    nodes: payloadNodes,\n+    default_node_ids: payloadNodes.filter((node) => node.catalog.default).map((node) => node.expected_manifest_id),\n+    mandatory_node_ids: payloadNodes.filter((node) => node.catalog.mandatory).map((node) => node.expected_manifest_id),\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch\n"
}

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-website/src/features/agentNodes/submissions.ts\n+import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase';\n+import { slugify } from '@/lib/routing';\n+\n+export interface AgentNodeSubmissionInput {\n+  ownerUserId: string;\n+  name: string;\n+  nodeType: 'agent' | 'orchestrator';\n+  shortDescription: string;\n+  description: string;\n+  repoUrl: string;\n+  expectedManifestId: string;\n+  creatorDiscordId: string;\n+  creatorDisplayName: string;\n+  manifestPath: string;\n+  refKind: 'branch' | 'tag' | 'commit_sha' | 'source_ref';\n+  refValue: string;\n+}\n+\n+export async function createAgentNodeSubmission(input: AgentNodeSubmissionInput): Promise<{ id: string; slug: string }> {\n+  if (!supabase) {\n+    throw new Error('Supabase is not configured.');\n+  }\n+\n+  const slug = `${slugify(input.name)}-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;\n+  const { data: node, error: nodeError } = await supabase\n+    .from('agent_nodes')\n+    .insert({\n+      owner_user_id: input.ownerUserId,\n+      slug,\n+      name: input.name,\n+      node_type: input.nodeType,\n+      short_description: input.shortDescription || null,\n+      description: input.description || null,\n+      repo_url: input.repoUrl,\n+      expected_manifest_id: input.expectedManifestId,\n+      creator_discord_id: input.creatorDiscordId || null,\n+      creator_display_name: input.creatorDisplayName || null,\n+      is_public: true,\n+    })\n+    .select('id, slug')\n+    .single();\n+\n+  if (nodeError || !node) {\n+    throw new Error(nodeError?.message || 'Failed to create art agent submission.');\n+  }\n+\n+  const { error: catalogError } = await supabase\n+    .from('agent_node_catalog_metadata')\n+    .insert({\n+      agent_node_id: node.id,\n+      review_status: 'pending',\n+      is_catalog_enabled: false,\n+    });\n+\n+  if (catalogError) {\n+    throw new Error(catalogError.message);\n+  }\n+\n+  const refColumns = {\n+    branch: null as string | null,\n+    tag: null as string | null,\n+    commit_sha: null as string | null,\n+    source_ref: null as string | null,\n+    [input.refKind]: input.refValue,\n+  };\n+\n+  const { error: targetError } = await supabase\n+    .from('agent_node_install_targets')\n+    .insert({\n+      agent_node_id: node.id,\n+      source_type: 'git',\n+      repo_url: input.repoUrl,\n+      manifest_path: input.manifestPath,\n+      expected_node_id: input.expectedManifestId,\n+      is_enabled: false,\n+      ...refColumns,\n+    });\n+\n+  if (targetError) {\n+    throw new Error(targetError.message);\n+  }\n+\n+  return node;\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}
